# Unallocated R11 source lead: positive interval modes for a variable physical word

**UNVERIFIED manual derivation; the positive interval dictionary is REFUTED at its stated certificate scope by Section5. No model/program allocation or target certificate.** The source check was for proof methods, not a posted solution of the exact original benchmark. The original Goal remains OPEN. No new mathematical execution was used.

[Jinze Zhao, Constant Steps Are s-Composable, arXiv:2609.11784v1](https://arxiv.org/html/2609.11784v1), Sections3.1--3.3, constructs positive interval-supported interpolation matrices whose product with a constant-step trajectory matrix has diagonal symmetric part. Its theorem concerns a specially balanced CONSTANT schedule; it does not establish a variable-word or perturbed-Silver theorem. The identities below are a manual variable-step extension of that matrix mechanism, not an import of its positivity theorem for combined weights.

## 1. Exact matrix mode for an arbitrary positive word

Let the physical word be h_0,...,h_(n-1), with h_i>=1 for this mode construction. Let B be the (n+1)-square trajectory matrix

    B_ij=h_j for j<i, B_ii=1, B_ij=0 for j>i.

Fix a contiguous interval [a,b] of query indices, a<b. Choose positive weights z_i,v_i for i in[a,b], and set

    Z_i=sum_(a<=k<=i) z_k,  Z_i^-=sum_(a<=k<i) z_k,
    V_i^+=sum_(i<k<=b) v_k, V_i=v_i+V_i^+,
    u_i=h_i Z_i-z_i, w_i=v_i+h_i V_i^+.

The value h_b is irrelevant wherever b is the terminal query: u_b has no upper entries and w_b=v_b. It can be given any dummy value without changing the matrix. Define

    C_ij=u_i v_j for a<=i<j<=b,
    C_ij=w_i z_j for a<=j<i<=b,
    C_ii=-sum_(j!=i) C_ij,

with entries outside the interval zero. Every off-diagonal entry is nonnegative, because u_i=h_i Z_i^-+(h_i-1)z_i>=0 and w_i>0. Every row sums tozero.

For i<j in the interval,

    (CB)_ij=u_i(v_j+h_j V_j^+)=u_i w_j,
    (CB)_ji=w_j(z_i-h_i Z_i)=-w_j u_i.

The second equality uses the zero row sum. Entries of CB outside the interval vanish: earlier columns are h_j times a zero row sum, and later columns are zero by triangularity. Therefore H=CB+B^T C^T is DIAGONAL, with

    H_ii=2[(h_i-1)^2 Z_i V_i^+ - Z_i^- V_i].        (1)

For i=b this is -2 Z_b^- v_b, independent of the dummy h_b. To check(1),

    (CB)_ii=(h_i-1)u_i V_i^+ - w_i Z_i^-
            =(h_i-1)^2 Z_i V_i^+ - Z_i^- V_i.

With constant h and z=v=1 this reproduces the source's interval mode. For variable h it keeps the ACTUAL physical trajectory matrix, not a reparameterized clock. No smoothness or interpolation condition is changed.

## 2. Exact smooth-convex inequality represented by the mode

Let bvec denote the column-sum vector of C, so sum bvec=0. For actual normalized smooth-convex data define

    Q_ij=2f_i-2f_j-2<g_j,x_i-x_j>-||g_i-g_j||²>=0.

Then lambda_ij=C_ji>=0 for i!=j, and the exact identity is

    sum_(i,j) C_ji Q_ij
       =2 sum_i bvec_i f_i + sum_i(H_ii-bvec_i)||g_i||².   (2)

Indeed beta_i=(Bg)_i=g_i+x0-x_i gives

    Q_ij=2f_i-2f_j+2<g_j,beta_i-beta_j>-||g_i||²+||g_j||².

Summing the full zero-row-sum C, including its harmless diagonal sinceQ_ii=0, eliminates the beta_j terms and leaves2g^T C B g. Equation(1) makes this diagonal. This also fixes the transpose convention; using C_ij rather thanC_ji as the interpolation multiplier would give a different expression.

Positive sums of these modes remain valid. However, no mode weights have been constructed that match the actual original-target objective on H_m. Nonnegativity of EACH mode does not imply nonnegativity of the coefficients needed to combine them.

## 3. A concrete sufficient whole-word target, not a claimed solution

For mass S=sum h and kappa=16/17, let

    sigma=2kappa-1=15/17, tau=1+sigma S,
    B_terminal=tau(tau-1)=sigma S(1+sigma S).

The actual smooth-convex history expression

    E_history=2 sum_(i<n) h_i(f_i-f_n)
              -sum_(i<n)h_i(h_i-1)||g_i||²
              -B_terminal||g_n||²                         (3)

would be nonnegative if an explicit nonnegative combination of modes yielded column sums

    bvec_i=h_i for i<n, bvec_n=-S,

and diagonal entries satisfying

    H_ii=h_i(2-h_i)-mu_i for i<n,
    H_nn=-S-B_terminal-mu_n,
    mu_i>=0 for EVERY index.

Then(2) plus sum mu_i||g_i||² equals(3). This is a checkable sufficient coefficient target with real physical steps. It is NOT a solved coefficient problem or a necessity theorem.

For a selected original minimizer, (3) plus sum_(i<n)h_i Q_star,i gives

    ||xn-xstar||²+2S(f_n-fstar)+B_terminal||g_n||²
          <=||x0-xstar||².

Equivalently, u_i=h_i for i<n andu_n=tau give a Zhang--Jiang dominance residual, with total weight

    sum u_i=S+tau=1+2kappa S.

To see this directly, use Q_star,i=2fstar-2f_i+2<g_i,x_i-xstar>-||g_i||² in the NONNEGATIVE interpolation convention above. The doubled dominance residual is

    ||x0-xstar||²-sum_i u_i Q_star,i
        -2(sum u_i)(f_n-fstar)-||x0-xstar-sum_i u_i g_i||².

Its exact expansion equals(3), because every cross-gradient coefficientu_i(h_j-u_j),j<i, vanishes. The minus sign in front ofsum u_i Q_star,i is essential with this nonnegative convention. Nonnegativity of(3) would thus imply the full-class value bound

    W_n(h)<=1/[1+(32/17)S] <=17/[16(1+2S)].

No such nonnegative combination for the complete inheritedH_m is supplied here. The positive-mode dictionary and the exact coefficient target must not be described as an upper certificate.

## 4. Required novelty and scope checks before any new allocation

The old R2 dense A/D diagonal-history family, even with arbitrary nonnegative diagonal slack and terminal penalty, was excluded on its stated scope. This mode dictionary must be compared with that actual family before being treated as a new route; no claim of strict containment or escape is made. The R7 all-level q_j terminal potential was actually false, so a reduced coefficient cannot simply be propagated by the primitive join theorem. The uniform B_terminal here is a distinct sufficient candidate, not a theorem or an approved repair.

A useful continuation would have to give actual nonnegative interval coefficients/slack on ALL relevant physical words, or show a sharp obstruction to this precise dictionary. Returning only the linear feasibility conditions above repeats an unproved certificate problem. This note carries NO new model, mathematical-program or formal allowance. The source's constant-step theorem, the Moreau/proximal objective criterion, and its balanced rate identity do not resolve the original Zhang--Jiang profile target.

## 5. Completed novelty check: the conic hull still has the central-bridge obstruction

After deriving Sections1--4, the parent read COMPLETE Sections2--4 of research/dense_history_search_20261008_r2/REPORT.md and its ROOT_DERIVATION_UNVERIFIED.md. Each interval mode above is an atom of that older A/D family. Specifically, extend

    A_k=sum_(a<=i<=min(k,b)) z_i, withA_k=0 beforea,
    D_k=sum_(max(k,a)<=i<=b) v_i, withD_k=0 afterb.

Then, for i<j,

    C_ji=(A_i-A_(i-1))(D_j+(h_j-1)D_(j+1)),
    C_ij=(A_(i-1)+(h_i-1)A_i)(D_j-D_(j+1)).        (4)

The terminal h_n is bookkeeping only; its term multipliesD_(n+1)=0. Weak monotonicity, including A_0=0 for intervals that start later, is enough. Formula(4) matches every original off-diagonal entry, not merely a terminal scalar.

A positive SUM of modes need not itself factor into one pairA,D. However the old central-cut proof extends to its entire finite positive conic hull. This requires a separate argument, given here rather than assuming that one-atom exclusion implies sum exclusion.

Index the atoms bynu and absorb their nonnegative combination weights intoA^nu. Define

    Z_k=sum_nu A_k^nu D_(k+1)^nu,
    q_k=1+2sum_nu A_(k-1)^nu D_k^nu,
    T_k=sum_(i<k)h_i,
    B_k^nu=sum_(i<k)h_i A_i^nu,
    E_k^nu=sum_(i>=k, i<n)h_i D_(i+1)^nu.

If the combined function coefficients are bvec_i=h_i fori<n andbvec_n=-S, the cut equations are exactly

    sum_nu[A_k^nu E_k^nu-D_(k+1)^nu B_k^nu
                         -2(h_k-1)A_k^nu D_(k+1)^nu]
       =T_k+h_k.                                       (5)

The physical diagonal residuals are

    mu_k=q_k-(h_k-1)^2 q_(k+1).

The same increment calculation as in the older prefix identity, now summing each bilinear atom product, proves

    q_k+sum_(i<k)mu_i
       =(T_k+1)^2-2sum_nu B_k^nu E_k^nu.               (6)

For clarity, B_(k+1)^nu=B_k^nu+h_k A_k^nu and E_(k+1)^nu=E_k^nu-h_k D_(k+1)^nu. The increment of the right side of(6), using(5), is[1-(h_k-1)^2]q_(k+1), exactly the increment on the left. Both sides start at1. Thus(6) needs only the COMBINED cut equations, not the false assumption that each atom separately matches them.

Since each B_k^nu E_k^nu>=0, nonnegative physical residuals implyq_k<=(T_k+1)^2. Now consider a palindrome with equal left/right masses s and middle physicalstep1+r, r>0, atindexk. SetC=s+1. Monotonicity givesE_(k+1)^nu<=s D_(k+1)^nu in EACH atom. The central cut(5), with the bridge separated from its right suffix, therefore gives

    C+r=sum_nu[A_k^nu E_(k+1)^nu+(1-r)A_k^nuD_(k+1)^nu
                                -D_(k+1)^nu B_k^nu]
         <=(C-r) Z_k.                                 (7)

The left side is positive, so C>r and Z_k>0. Hence

    q_(k+1)=1+2Z_k >=(3C+r)/(C-r).

At the same physical bridge, mu_k>=0 and(6) imply

    r² q_(k+1)<=q_k<=C².

With x=r/C in(0,1),

    x²(3+x)/(1-x)<=1,
    (x+1)(x²+2x-1)<=0,
    r/(s+1)<=sqrt2-1.                                 (8)

The weak inequality is sufficient. No strict-positivity assumption on every atom at every query was used.

The actual inherited central bridge violates(8). Withc0=1+sqrt2,epsilon=1/(4m),beta=1-epsilon,c=c0(1+epsilon),a=beta/c andj=m-1,

    r/(s+1)=(c-2)/[1+tau(2/c)^j],
    tau=[c(c-2)-beta]/beta,
    0<tau<9epsilon.

Forj>=3, (1/c0)*tau*(2/c)^j<72epsilon/c0^4<c0epsilon becausec0^5=41+29sqrt2>72. Sincec-2=1/c0+c0epsilon, this givesr/(s+1)>1/c0. Allm>=1536 havej>=3. Thus the entire positive conic hull of the variable-step interval modes is infeasible for the desired physical coefficient/diagonal-sign target, BEFORE imposing any terminal penalty.

This is a complete manual obstruction to the stated dictionary, not a full-PEP impossibility and not a counterexample to(3). It includes arbitrary finite mode sets, arbitrary positive z,v within intervals, arbitrary nonnegative combination weights, and arbitrary nonnegative physical diagonal slack. It does not include a signed mode expansion whose FINAL interpolation entries remain nonnegative, arbitrary nonseparable matrices not in this cone, non-diagonal PSD remainders, or different function-value weights.

No new model or mathematical program should be allocated to retuning POSITIVE weights in this dictionary. The source-derived algebra is valid, but its positive-sum route repeats the old central obstruction in a larger, now explicitly treated scope.

# R12 original calls complete: fixed A/B rejected, Profile C open

2026-10-09. **UNVERIFIED draft mathematics. Original Goal ACTIVE/OPEN.** Both original R12 calls are complete and parent-full-read/hash-checked. A later Profile C allocation, if separately registered, is not a renewal of either old grant.

Original target: lim W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1 or a certified full-class asymptotic separation, W=2R, exact all-integer B_n, every nonnegative predetermined word, all finite dimensions, original selected minimizer/radius and actual endpoint. No original-target proof/disproof or complete candidate exists.

## Exact fixed-profile result

For each enclosing m>=1536, keep epsilon=1/(4m),beta=1-epsilon,c=(1+sqrt2)(1+epsilon),a=beta/c and H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a c^j,H_j), with the SAME parameters in every child. LetS=sumH_m,T_i=sum_(t<i)h_t andBcert=1+(32/17)S. Bcert is not the original benchmarkB_n.

The fixed physical profiles were rho_i^A=1-T_i/(2S) andrho_i^B=(S+1)/(S+1+T_i). For either, u_i=rho_i h_i(i<n),u_n=Bcert-sum_(i<n)u_i. The original doubled dominance functional is

    D_u=||x0-xstar||²-sum_i u_i Q_star,i
        -2Bcert(f_n-fstar)-||x0-xstar-sum_i u_i g_i||²,

with NONNEGATIVEQ_star,i. It is strictly negative for BOTH fixed profiles on every sufficiently large ODD complete enclosing depthm, on one fixed scalar Huber function. This rules out EVERY admissible matrixC whose original interpolation plus FULLPSDremainder would represent that fixed functional. It does not rule out the objective upper bound itself.

Usef(x)=x²/2 for|x|<=1,|x|-1/2 otherwise, withuniqueoriginalminimum0 andgradientclip[-1,1]. Writek_j=a c^j,s_j=sumH_j and

    X1=beta, X_j=s_(j-1)+1+k_(j-1)-X_(j-1),j>=2,
    x0=X_m, x_n=g_n=(-1)^m t, t=a beta.

The complete positivity induction provesk_j>=X_j>0. Every leftchild/bridge is saturated with its current sign and lands at the negative next critical start. All physical queries are included; only the last physical query has gradient magnitude beta rather than1. EveryQ_star,i=0; the ORIGINALradius isX_m².

Forr=sqrt2-1,v=r²/(1+2sqrt2), the literal enclosing-m limits are

    X_m/S->r, V_m/S²->v, M_m/S²->v,
    V_m=sum h_i², M_m=sum h_i x_(i+1).

The source proofs provide exact finite-depth formulas and uniform geometric error bounds; no fixed-internal-depth limit is substituted. Profile A satisfies

    U_A=3S/4+V_m/(4S),
    G_Aphys=X_m-x_n/2-M_m/(2S),
    Z_A=sum_(i=0)^n u_i^A g_i,
    Z_A/S -> -9r/68-(2-r)v/4<0   (oddm).

For PHYSICAL i<n only,

    0<=rho_i^A-rho_i^B
      =T_i(S-1-T_i)/[2S(S+1+T_i)]<=r²/2.

The last physicalstep>1 makes the index restriction valid. The ordering is reversed at terminalrho_n, which is not used to compare physical sums. Negative physical gradientmass/S tends(1-r)/2, yielding

    limsup_(oddm) Z_B/S < -19r/272<0.

ThusD_u=2X_m Z-Z²-Bcert t²<0. For any nonnegative original interpolation coefficientsC_ji with required row/column conditions,

    g^T R(C)g=D_u-sum C_ji Q_ij<=D_u<0.

The same ACTUAL gradient vector excludes FULLPSD for everyC; no diagonal restriction, old A/D ansatz, or free-vector screen is involved. The two independent derivations agree; that agreement is not Danus verifier acceptance.

Crucial scope: the actual objective payoff ist²/X_m²=Theta(S^-2), andS²payoff->1. It does NOT attain the targetS^-1 scale or refute a full-class upper boundW(H_m)<=1/Bcert. The failure is the profiles' prescribed residual square.

## Profile C and its necessary calibration

The later exact addendum R12_PROFILE_C_ADDENDUM_20261009.md hasSHA9001df1d3b8ecdbc198d8be2e85657610263fc93fdf8fd50f3e295f058e58030. It fixesrho_i=1-T_i/(8S), with the sameBcert andterminalmassrule.

For the entire linearfamilyrho_i=1-theta*T_i/S, the same criticalorbit gives

    Z_theta/S ->2r/17-theta*(3sqrt2-4)  (oddm).

Thus everyfixedtheta>(2+sqrt2)/17 is rejected. Profile C(theta1/8) has positive margin(52-35sqrt2)/136>1/68 on that test and also passes its even-depth counterpart. This is only a necessary calibration; no validity for other histories or a full PSDcertificate follows.

The primal call used its previously unused second approach on a fixed regular triangle with proposed cyclic vertices. Its actual required root normal has

    <n_m,v1-v0>/S_(m-1) ->3r²/[2(1+r+r²)]>0,

so the proposed full vertex table is infeasible. The true departure fromv0 occurs when the physical cumulative time exceeds an explicitthresholdtau_m withtau_m/S_(m-1)->1-r²/(1+r+r²). No subsequent actual triangular endpoint, payoff orD_Csign was derived. This is not a Profile C counterexample.

# Unallocated next lead: excess physical weights and a Laplacian remainder

**UNVERIFIED manual algebra; no complete certificate or new allocation.** The original Goal remains OPEN. This note defines a concrete alternative to the decreasing-clock profiles. It does not declare Profile C false or solve the original target.

## 1. A source-grounded motivation, with its exact boundary

In the canonical primitive--dominant concatenation of [Zhang--Jiang, Section3.2](https://arxiv.org/html/2410.12395v2), the left physical weights are the left steps, the bridge weight isgamma=s_left+2, and the right dominant vector is multiplied bytau>1. The physical bridge isbeta=1+(s_left+1)/tau<=gamma. Consequently the canonical recursively constructed dominant vector satisfiesu_i>=h_i at every PHYSICAL index. This is a property of those source-constructed schedules and weights; it does not make the actual perturbedH_m dominant.

The failed A/B profiles instead reduced physical weights and assigned the missing total to the terminal gradient. Their actual counterexample shows why that terminal residual square can be too large. The following fixed candidate puts more mass on physical gradients and less at the terminal query.

## 2. Explicit increasing-clock candidate

For the literal inheritedH_m letS=sumh,T_i=sum_(t<i)h_t andBcert=1+(32/17)S. Set

    u_i=(1+T_i/(2S))h_i, i<n,
    u_n=Bcert-sum_(i<n)u_i.                          (1)

The physical weights satisfyu_i>=h_i. WithV=sum h_i²,

    sum_(i<n)u_i=5S/4-V/(4S),
    u_n>=1+(43/68)S>0.

The strict inequality usesV>=0; no additional physical step is introduced. The desired total remainsBcert, distinct from the original benchmarkB_n.

Use NONNEGATIVEQ_star,i and define the original doubled dominance functional

    D_u=||x0-xstar||²-sum_i u_i Q_star,i
        -2Bcert(f_n-fstar)-||x0-xstar-sum_i u_i g_i||².

For any nonnegativeu, its exact gradient expansion is

    D_u=2sum_(i<n)u_i(f_i-f_n)
      +sum_i u_i(1-u_i)||g_i||²
      +2sum_(i<j)u_j(h_i-u_i)<g_i,g_j>.              (2)

Every h_i in the last sum is physical, becausei<j<=n.

## 3. Exact positive gradient-difference energy

Fori<j definew_ij=u_j(u_i-h_i)>=0. LetL be the graph Laplacian with off-diagonalL_ij=-w_ij and diagonalL_ii=sum_(j!=i)w_(min(i,j),max(i,j)). Then

    g^T L g=sum_(i<j) w_ij||g_i-g_j||²>=0.

The off-diagonal coefficients of(2) are EXACTLY those ofL. For any zero-row-sum interpolation matrixC with nonnegative off-diagonal entries and column sumsb_i=u_i(i<n),b_n=u_n-Bcert, putH=C Bmat+Bmat^T C^T using the ACTUAL trajectory matrix. The exact full remainder is

    R=L+diag(u_i(1-u_i)+b_i-L_ii)-H,
    D_u=sum_(i,j)C_ji Q_ij+g^T R g.                  (3)

This is a deterministic original-gradient identity. IfH is diagonal, the remainder is a positive gradient-difference energy plus a signed diagonal; nonnegative individual diagonal entries would be sufficient but are not assumed necessary. IfH is not diagonal, its whole contribution must be retained. NeitherL>=0 nor the mass identity in(1) provesR>=0.

Sinceu_i-[h_i,0]_i is componentwise nonnegative, the residual vector can also be written

    x0-sum_i u_i g_i=x_n-sum_i(u_i-[h_i,0]_i)g_i.

For projection envelopes, the latter excess-weighted gradient divided by its totalBcert-S is a genuine convex combination in the SAMEbody. This may provide a history-dependent comparison point. It is not an actual extra query or permission to change the function.

## 4. Exact critical-orbit calibration, not a validity claim

The complete symmetric-Huber orbit in the R12 A/B counterexample hasr=sqrt2-1,v=r²/(1+2sqrt2), X_m/S->r andM_m/S²->v, whereM_m=sum h_i x_(i+1). Generalizing(1) to u_i=(1+theta*T_i/S)h_i withtheta>0, finite summation by parts gives

    U_phys=S+theta*(S²-V)/(2S),
    G_phys=X_m-(1+theta)x_n+theta*M_m/S.

At odd depths, whereg_n=x_n->-r, the totalZ=sumu_i g_i satisfies

    Z/S ->2r/17+theta*(3sqrt2-4).

At even depths,

    Z/S ->r(1+15/17)-theta*(5sqrt2-6)/7.

For the fixedtheta1/2 in(1), both limits lie strictly between0 and2r. SinceBcert*f_n/S²->0, the leading dominance residual on this ONEorbit is positive at both parities. This proves only that the known A/B counterexample does not reject the new candidate. Other functions, starts and dimensions have not been checked.

## 5. Actual remaining proof obligation

A continuation must choose explicitC and prove the FULLmatrixR in(3) PSD, uniformly over every sufficiently large inheritedm, or produce a different actual complete-wordD_u<0 witness. A genuine fullPSDproof would implyW(H_m)<=1/Bcert and close the conditional original-target separation through accepted931b9653ce0f7201. No such proof exists in this note.

The acyclic-flow diagonal obstruction still applies whenU_phys=O(S) andthe centralu_i is orderS. The single nonnegative destination-law obstruction in R12_PARENT_RANKONE_FLOW_OBSTRUCTION_20261009.md also applies because every fixed earlyu_i tends to its step andU_phys->infinity. Thus simple causal transport or retuning one global destination distribution cannot establish this candidate. A usefulC needs genuinely index-dependent circulations or another explicit original-space construction.

The old positive-interval-cone result fixed b_i=h_i and separately nonnegative physical diagonal slack; it cannot be transferred automatically to(3). Conversely, merely renaming its feasibility system using a Laplacian is not progress. The candidate has NOmodel, mathematical-program or formal allowance attached.

## Fixed A/B profiles: complete actual all-depth rejection

ReadR12_AB_COUNTEREXAMPLE_HANDOFF_20261009.md and its two complete source proofs. WithS=sumH_m,T_i=sum_(t<i)h_t,Bcert=1+(32/17)S, the fixed profiles were

For PHYSICALi<n only, 0<=rho_A-rho_B<=r²/2, while the negative-gradient mass/S tends(1-r)/2. This yieldslimsupZ_B/S<-19r/272<0. The ordering ofrho reverses at terminaln; it was not used there. SinceD_u=2X_m Z-Z²-Bcert*(a beta)², both actual residuals are negative.

Their actual objective payoff isTheta(S^-2), andS²payoff->1. This is NOT a benchmark-scale lower witness and does not disproveW(H_m)<=1/Bcert. It rejects only the prescribed dominance square. The two independently derived proofs agree but are not verifier-accepted facts.

## Profile C remains open

Use the exact full dominance remainderR(C), not separate signs ofR-K. For any admissibleC, physicalnodei withbackwardoutgoingP_i=sum_(j<i)C_ij andforwardF_i=sum_(j>i)C_ij has

## New unallocated source-grounded lead

# Parent R12 extension: every single nonnegative destination law fails

**UNVERIFIED manual proof. This excludes a coefficient family, not Profile C or the original minimax problem.** No new model or mathematical program was used. The R12b report checked one explicit rank-one flow; this note proves the stated larger single-law scope without changing that report's bytes.

Let the physical source weights be u_i>0 fori<n and letU=sum_(i<n)u_i. The required column sums areb_i=u_i physically andb_n=-U. Consider ANY matrix

    C=p q^T-diag(p), p_i>=0,q_i>=0,sum q_i=1,

whose column sums areb. WriteP=sum p_i. Then

    p_i=P q_i-u_i (i<n), p_n=P q_n+U,
    P>=U.

There is no restriction to q_i proportional to the algebraicell_i. Both p andq may depend arbitrarily on the whole word.

For a physical nodei letA_i=sum_(j<i)q_j andF_i=sum_(j>i)q_j. The FULL dominance remainder has diagonal

    R_ii=u_i(2-u_i)-2p_i[(h_i-1)F_i-A_i].          (1)

This follows from the actual lower-triangular trajectory matrix and already includes the complete covariance or other exact decomposition of the dominance cross terms.

Suppose along a sequence of growing words thatU->infinity, that there is a FIXED physical indexJ, and that for0<=i<=J:

- theu_i remain bounded;
- h_i-1 is bounded below by one positive constant;
- u_J>2 by one fixed positive margin for all sufficiently large words.

Then no such single-lawC can haveR PSD for all sufficiently large words. Proof follows.

AssumeR_ii>=0 for every0<=i<=J. Inductively prove thatp_i remains bounded for each such fixedi, or obtain a contradiction already at that node. Ifp_j is bounded forj<i, then

    P A_i=sum_(j<i)(p_j+u_j)=O(1),

andP>=U->infinity. The physical weight strictly afteri isU_after=U-sum_(j<=i)u_j, which tends to infinity. Nonnegativity ofp_j gives

    P F_i>=U_after.

Ifq_i>=1/2 along an unbounded subsequence, thenp_i=Pq_i-u_i>=P/2-O(1), whilep_i<=P. Hence

    2p_i[(h_i-1)F_i-A_i]
      >=(1-o(1)) r_min U_after-O(1) ->infinity.

This contradicts(1), becauseu_i(2-u_i) is bounded above. Thusq_i<1/2 eventually. SinceA_i=O(1/P), nowF_i=1-A_i-q_i>=1/2-o(1), and

    (h_i-1)F_i-A_i>=r_min/3>0

for all sufficiently large words. Equation(1) forcesp_i to be bounded whenu_i(2-u_i)>=0; if that latter number is negative, it already gives a contradiction. This proves the induction. AtindexJ, u_J>2 makesu_J(2-u_J)<0, so the contradiction necessarily occurs byJ.

For Profile C on the literal inheritedH_m, U>=7S_m/8->infinity. At each fixed early indexrho_i->1, henceu_i->h_i^0. The first four physical steps converge to

    sqrt2, 2, sqrt2, 1+(1+sqrt2).

Thus J=3 satisfies every hypothesis, includingu_3->2+sqrt2>2. The entire nonnegative single-destination-law class is therefore excluded at sufficiently large enclosingm, even ifq is retuned as a function ofm. The same argument applies to other clock profiles with these explicitly stated early-weight and total-source limits.

This does not exclude sums with different destination laws, general state-dependent circulations, arbitrary nonseparableC, or the possibility thatD_u>=0 has a different certificate. No negative direction here is asserted to be a realizable function trajectory. The result is only a necessary full-matrix diagnostic for the named coefficient family.

## Actual critical Huber test and the exact parameter threshold

Use the ONE symmetric Huber functionF(x)=x²/2 for|x|<=1 and|x|-1/2 otherwise, with original minimum0. Letk_j=a c^j,s_j=sumH_j and

# Unallocated parent lead: a quantitatively relaxed primitive--dominant join

**UNVERIFIED manual derivation. Original target OPEN.** This is a complete conditional joining calculation with a proved scalar PSD margin. The required all-depth left history inequality is NOT established. No new model/program/formal allocation is attached. The calculation adapts the retained interpolation mechanism of [Zhang--Jiang, Section3.2](https://arxiv.org/html/2410.12395v2), keeping its actual hypotheses explicit.

## 1. Exact full-class input certificates

Normalize L=1 and keep one convex1-smooth function with an ORIGINAL selected minimizer. Translate that minimizer to0 and its value to0 for notation only. Use NONNEGATIVE rows

    Q_ij=2f_i-2f_j-2<g_j,x_i-x_j>-||g_i-g_j||²,
    Q_star,i=-2f_i+2<g_i,x_i>-||g_i||².

A left physical word of masss runs x->v, p=grad f(v). Its retained weak primitive input is

    E_L=||x||²-sum_left h_i Q_star,i
            -2s f(v)-||v||²-Gamma||p||²>=0.          (1)

Equivalently this is the actual history expression

    E_L=2sum_left h_i(f_i-f(v))
          -sum_left h_i(h_i-1)||g_i||²-Gamma||p||².

After the physical bridgeb=1+k, w=v-bp, a right physical word runsw->y. Let fixed nonnegative right weights have totalB_R>0 and weighted gradientU=sum_right u_i g_i, including the actual terminalquery. Its retained weak dominance input is

    D_R=||w||²-sum_right u_i Q_star,i-2B_R f(y)
                                -||w-U||²>=0.       (2)

The right physical mass need not equal(B_R-1)/2. Both(1) and(2) must actually hold for all histories on their specified words. They cannot be inferred from scalar capacities alone.

Choosegamma>=0,tau>=0 and parent sourcevector

    u_parent=(h_left, gamma, tau*u_right),
    B_P=s+gamma+tau B_R.

Every weight remains attached to an actual physical or terminal query.

## 2. The exact retained joining identity

LetA_Q=sum_right u_i Q_(v,i)>=0, wherev denotes the actual bridge query. The row identity

    Q_(v,i)=Q_star,i+2f(v)-||p||²+2<g_i,p-v>

and(2) give EXACTLY

    2B_R[f(v)-f(y)]
       =B_R||p||²+2k<p,U>+||U||²+D_R+A_Q.          (3)

The doubled parent dominance residual expands to

    D_P=E_L+[tau+(s+gamma)/B_R]D_R
         +[(s+gamma)/B_R]A_Q
         +Aq||p||²+2Bq<p,U>+Cq||U||²,              (4)

where

    Aq=Gamma+s+2gamma-gamma²,
    Bq=(s+gamma)k/B_R+tau(b-gamma),
    Cq=(s+gamma)/B_R-tau(tau-1).                    (5)

To check(4), first use(1) to expose the left residual and(2) to obtain

    D_P=E_L+tau D_R+2(s+gamma)[f(v)-f(y)]
          +(Gamma+gamma-gamma²)||p||²
          +2tau(b-gamma)<p,U>-tau(tau-1)||U||².

Substitution of(3) gives(4). Thus if the two-by-two scalar matrix with entriesAq,Bq,Cq is PSD, the original parent dominance residual is nonnegative. The ACTUAL child residuals and bridge-to-right interpolation rows have positive coefficients; they are not silently replaced by primitive positivity at higher levels.

This is a full smooth-convex statement, not just the projection subclass at selected0. Translating the original minimum back restores its original radius without changing any physical step.

## 3. A uniform PSD margin on the actual inherited hierarchy

Take the actual parentH_(j+1) withj>=1 andsame enclosingm>=1536. Its equal childmass iss=S_j, capacityC=1+s, bridgeexcessk=a c^j andratio r=k/C. Elementary inherited mass bounds give

    C>7/3, 2/5<r<5/12.                              (6)

For the lower ratio useC<=c^j and a>2/5; the latter follows fromepsilon<=1/6144 andc0<17/7. For the upper usek/C<=c-2<5/12. These are actual finite-parameter bounds, not limits.

Setkappa=16/17, useB_R=1+2kappa s, and prescribeB_P=1+2kappa(2s+b). Choose

    gamma=1+(3/4)C,
    tau=[B_P-s-gamma]/B_R,
    Gamma >=(7/10)C²-C.                             (7)

DefineD0=4(32-15/C) andN=9+128r. Then

    tau=1+N/D0>1,
    Aq/C²>=11/80,
    Cq=(119-N)/D0-N²/D0²,
    Bq/C=r-3/4+(128r²+32r-27/4)/D0.                (8)

Here716/7<D0<128. Cq decreases withr. At its worst r=5/12 it is increasing inD0 throughout this interval, since2(187/3)²-(170/3)*128=4658/9>0. Consequently

    Cq >842639/4613904 >9/50.                       (9)

The last comparison is the positive integer inequality42131950>41525136. The expressionBq/C increases withr and decreases withD0 on(6), giving

    -1827/12800 < Bq/C < -1333/25776 <0,
    |Bq|<C/7.                                      (10)

Indeed1827*7=12789<12800. Therefore

    Aq*Cq-Bq² >[(11/80)(9/50)-1/49]C²
                  =(851/196000)C²>0.               (11)

Both diagonal entries are positive. Thus the joining matrix is uniformly positive definite. In additiongamma>=b, becauser<5/12<3/4; if the right physical weights satisfyu_i>=h_i, the parent weights do too, sincetau>1 andtheleftweights are exactlyh_i.

## 4. The actual leaf inputs are available

For a single step1<h<=sqrt2, the exact history identity is

    E_(h(h+1))=(h+1)Q_01+Q_10+(2-h²)||g0-g1||²>=0.

HereE_Gamma=2h(f0-f1)-h(h-1)||g0||²-Gamma||g1||². DecreasingGamma preserves nonnegativity. This supplies(1) withGamma=(7/10)(1+h)²-(1+h), becauseh(h+1)-Gamma=(3/10)(1+h)².

The actual inherited leaf has1<h<sqrt2. For its weak dominance totalB_1=1+2kappa h, chooseweightsu0=h,u1=1+(2kappa-1)h. Their residual equalsE_(u1(u1-1)). Since0<2kappa-1<1,

    h(h+1)-u1(u1-1)
       =h[(1-sigma)+(1-sigma²)h]>=0,
    sigma=2kappa-1.

Thus this is also a genuine full-class leaf dominance input. No claim about higher history inputs follows from the leaf.

## 5. Precise unresolved all-depth premise

If, for every sufficiently large enclosingm and EVERY1<=j<m, the actual same-parameterH_j obeyed(1) with

    Gamma_j=(7/10)(1+S_j)²-(1+S_j),

on every full smooth-convex history, then(4)--(11) and the genuine leaf would recursively produce fixed nonnegative parent vectors with total1+(32/17)S_m. This would establish the full original-radius boundW(H_m)<=1/[1+(32/17)S_m], hence the original negative resolution through accepted931b9653ce0f7201.

That left history premise is NOT proved here. The old positive A/D interval cone cannot prove it under its diagonal-slack restrictions, regardless of terminalGamma. The R7 q_j potential was false at its stated stronger coefficients; it does not settle this new all-depth input. A successful next step must supply a genuinely non-diagonal/original-row proof or an actual all-depth obstruction to the displayedGamma_j statement, rather than rename it or tune a scalar without a composition proof.

The advantage established by this note is only the conditional join: it tolerates a three-tenths C² loss in the left terminal-gradient coefficient while retaining the true right dominance residual and a uniform positive quadratic margin. The missing input still has substantial mathematical content. No original-target result, formal acceptance or new resource allowance is claimed.

# R13 handoff: exact target open; the next join must use less than full weak-left retention

The Goal is ACTIVE and the original problem is OPEN. All new mathematics below is an UNVERIFIED manual draft. Both R13 native calls have finished, and the parent has read their complete proofs and handoffs and checked their hashes. There is no complete candidate for formal verification.

## Original target and sufficient negative route

Keep the exact original statement `lim W_n^*/U_n^ZJ = 1`, or a rigorous full-class asymptotic separation, with `W = 2R`, all integer splits in `B_n = 1/U_n^ZJ`, all finite dimensions, every original selected minimizer/radius, and the actual last iterate. The original PROBLEM SHA-256 remains `3ecc3159a696e8528ebc727776d42f657cfbc6842e9212b28c870fcd05edb89d`.

For each enclosing `m >= 1536`, every child uses the SAME parameters

    epsilon = 1/(4m), beta = 1-epsilon,
    c = (1+sqrt(2))(1+epsilon), a = beta/c,
    H_0 = (), H_(j+1) = (H_j, 1+a*c^j, H_j),
    S_j = sum H_j, C_j = 1+S_j, k_j = a*c^j.

Accepted `931b9653ce0f7201` supplies the benchmark implication: a full-class bound `W(H_m) <= 17/[16(1+2S_m)]` for all sufficiently large m gives a strict `799/800` subsequence separation. This upper premise remains unproved. Accepted `1e0bf3f41457d8e4` supplies finite-word projection transfer and the actual H3 law, not higher-depth positivity.

## The conditional PD join survives; its weak history input is still missing

The parent note `R12_RELAXED_PD_JOIN_LEAD_20261009.md`, SHA `f7a80cbc05ee2da4b168f61fbc307817550865fa94978fcbfe067f1dae125c09`, and the independent R13 calculation agree on the exact retained PD join. If every required H_j satisfies

    E_j = 2 sum h_i(f_i-f_end) - sum h_i(h_i-1)||g_i||^2
          - [(7/10) C_j^2-C_j] ||g_end||^2 >= 0,

then a dominance induction with total `1+(32/17)S_m` closes the sufficient negative route. The scalar join determinant exceeds `851 C_j^2/196000`. This is a conditional implication, not an established all-depth input.

The new history proof derives the complete three-vector original-row remainder, then gives an actual obstruction to all rules retaining the weak left residual with coefficient at least one. For child mass s, C=1+s, bridge excess k and r=k/C, use the static asymmetric Huber function with gradient `clamp(x,-r,1)` and original start C. The whole left child has gradient 1, the bridge lands at -k, the whole right child has gradient -r, and the original final point/gradient is -r. For theta=7/10,

    E_L = (1-theta) C^2,
    E_R = (1-theta) C^2 r^2,
    E_P = C^2 [1-theta {r(2+r)}^2].

At every actual internal j>=3, r>sqrt(2)-1, so E_P<E_L. Thus `E_P >= alpha E_L + beta_R E_R + nonnegative original rows + PSD` is impossible for alpha>=1 and beta_R>=0, even with arbitrary original rows and an unrestricted full PSD remainder. Taking j=m-2 gives unbounded inherited depth. Yet E_P>(29/100)C^2: the history assertion itself is NOT refuted.

The exact standard-history recurrence is

    E_P^std = E_L^std + B E_R^std
               + ell(A_v+A_y+Q_vy+k Q_yv)
               + Delta (||q||^2-||p||^2),
    ell = (C+k)/(C-k), B = 1+2ell,
    Delta = B k^2-C^2
          = C^2(1+r)(r^2+2r-1)/(1-r).

Here A_v and A_y sum the actual bridge-to-right and terminal-to-right interpolation rows with physical h weights. This identity was derived independently by parent and native agent. The signed right energy and the defect cannot be discarded.

## What the new positive result actually says

For EACH FIXED j, there are eta_j>0 and M_j<infinity such that for all m>=M_j and all actual H_j(m) histories,

    E_j >= (eta_j/2) sum_(i=0..2^j-1) ||g_i||^2.

At exact Silver parameters epsilon=0, the standard leaf and join are nonnegative. Equality plus terminal gradient zero recursively forces every gradient to be zero. Adding the weak coefficient's `(3/10) C_j^2 ||g_end||^2` produces strict positivity on every nonzero finite interpolation dataset. Normalize its finite gradient Gram matrix to trace one; the endpoint rows bound all function differences. Compactness and continuity prove the displayed statement uniformly over ambient dimension.

No estimate on eta_j or M_j is supplied. The quantifiers cannot be exchanged. A violation at m tending to infinity would require j(m) tending to infinity. This rejects the parent's speculative fixed-prefix absorbed negative-history mechanism, but leaves growing-depth versions open.

For the entire cone-projection class `f(x)=||P_K x||^2/2`, every physical prefix T satisfies `||x_T-z_star|| <= ||x_0-z_star||` for EVERY original `z_star in K^polar`. The proof uses gradient contraction by h-1, inherited prefix products `prod(h_i-1)<=1`, and summation by parts. It also applies to compact truncations with a nonbinding cap. It does not cover active caps or arbitrary nonhomogeneous bodies, and does not assert step-by-step distance decrease.

## Fixed increasing global weights: two scoped results

Keep S=S_m, T_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, Bcert=1+(32/17)S,

    u_i=(1+T_i/(2S))h_i (i<n), u_n=Bcert-sum_(i<n)u_i.

The positive cone of variable-step interval matrices is now excluded at these exact columns, including arbitrary finite positive sums and m-dependent supports/weights. The full diagonal, with the Laplacian retained, yields a central necessary condition whose enclosing-m limit is `1+r^3 <= 1+r/6`, r=sqrt(2)-1. It fails because `6r^2>1`. This is a new derivation for u_i, not an import of the old h_i-only cone result. It does not exclude all general cyclic C, or even every diagonal-H C outside that cone.

For the actual symmetric Huber function, `T_Hj([-k_j,k_j])` is contained in `[-a beta,a beta]`. At J=floor(m/2), allow any start `Y_J in [max(0,k_J-d_J),k_J]`, where `d_j=k_(j+1)-(S_j+1)`, and lift via `Y_(j+1)=S_j+1+k_j-Y_j`. These are complete actual H_m trajectories with arbitrary inner phases at growing depth. Their prescribed D_u/S_m^2 has a uniform strictly positive limit lower bound. Both parities and the original radii are retained. Hence this entire phase-changing Huber attempt fails to refute the profile. Other starts, functions and dimensions remain open.

## Recovery decision, resources, and evidence

The next constructive test should allow a coefficient strictly below one on the weak left residual and retain actual cross-left/right interpolation rows, or obtain a quantitative whole-tree coercivity estimate. Repeating the unit-left weak-history join, positive interval-cone tuning, or the covered Huber inner phases cannot close the goal. ## Actual joint-history construction and the precise useful match

Use F_K(x)=max_(g in K)(<x,g>-||g||^2/2), with one fixed compact gradient hull containing0. Its actual gradient is P_K(x), and every normal <x_i-g_i,g-g_i><=0 is checked at all generators.

## Negative-correlation simplex: complete realization, quantitative selection absent

For m phases, let C_i=1+ordinary block mass; for i<m choose a checkpoint1+d_i with d_i>0. Put C_m=1+tail mass. Choose A_i>0 with sum1/A_i=1, P_i=sum_(j>=i) A_j C_j and A_i d_i>=P_(i+1). Explicit gradients

The proof uses actual multiplicities and three frequency regimes: all factors positive, all negative, or the first negative level k. It proves S_j<=c^j-1 and S_j>=a*c^(j+1)/2. ## Common-normal replenishment: exact radius cost and a whole-word limitation

Use F_K(x)=max_(g in K)(<x,g>-||g||^2/2), K=conv(0,g_i), with every gradient query and the terminal sample retained. The changes are

Suppose the minimum-norm common-normal foot e/||e||^2 lies in conv(g_i). Writing g_i=w_i+q*nu, q=1/||e||, the tangential projections form another REAL static projection-gradient history for the SAME whole word, with0 in its gradient hull. The D=0 case is just the Huber term. This is an exact pointwise inequality, not an asymptotic approximation or a formal Gram direction. It follows from (y-(2S+1)q)^2>=0.

## Centered and path simplexes: what was genuinely selected, and what failed

The R5 centered simplex was completely rederived. With physical ordinary masses C_i-1, checkpoints1+d_i, A_i>0, sum1/A_i=1 and P_i=sum_(j>=i)A_j*C_j, feasibility is A_i*d_i>=P_(i+1). Put delta_i=d_i-P_(i+1)/A_i. Its exact data include

All-tight guards force X_end=w_M. For a single checkpoint, the best legal ratio gives value d^2/[C_1^2(2C_2-1)], while all-tight selection gives d^2/(C_1^2 C_2^2); their ratio tends to0 as C_2 grows. Every point in the final interval is reachable by an actual original start of radius<=R. Optimizing the terminal endpoint energy divided by R^2 is exactly the supremum over original starts, not a favorable renormalization. The recurrence itself is not a solved quantitative theorem.

The native then proved a quantitative whole-word envelope, including every occurrence in every subtree and ANY number of returns:

    A_n(R)<=max{R-S_m, aR-[S_m-(1+a)], a*beta}, B_n(R)<=a.

Proof: unroll every reflected contribution delta_t*B_t minus the entire remaining suffix mass; B_t<=delta_(t-1). Adjacent high/low levels bound the reflected contribution by a*d_j. A level-j bridge has an entire H_j after it, and its following leaf still has suffix mass S_j-(1+a). The induction S_j-(1+a)>=a*d_j for j>=2 pays every high-level excursion; level1 leaves only a*beta. All physical suffixes are retained; there is no selected-spine restriction.

It follows exactly that A_n(R)>=1 iff R>=S_m+1, and A_n(R)=R-S_m in that region. Every actual affine-terminal trajectory, however many times it previously returned, obeys

    2F(x_n)/|x0|^2 <= [2R-D_m]/R^2 <=1/D_m,
    R=|x0|,

with equality at x0=-D_m. The entire affine-terminal sector therefore has exact optimum1/D_m. Every R>=sqrt(D_m) is also bounded by1/D_m. For R<=1, every physical prefix stays quadratic, so the exact value is P_m^2<1/D_m.

The UNSOLVED region for this fixed one-sided function is the quadratic terminal sector with1<R<sqrt(D_m). With complete physical interval endpoints, write

    K_m=sup_(1<R<sqrt(D_m)) max{A_n(R)^2,B_n(R)^2}/R^2.

The proven sector bounds give E_F(H_m)=max{1/D_m,K_m}. No target-scale lower bound, and no full-family upper, for K_m was obtained. Merely naming K_m is not progress toward the minimax target. Even a successful liminf B_n*K_m>=1 would only pass this inherited-family viability test; the original arbitrary-word selection would still need proof. The whole finite-cap asymmetric-Huber family and higher-dimensional returning histories remain unexcluded.

## Parent manual bridge algebra retained for source fidelity, not a claimed closure

The parent manually derived the following identities during R7; they were sent to the upper call, but that call did not use them to claim a second proof line. They remain UNVERIFIED and must be checked before reuse.

For a word of massS define the history functional

    H_B=sum_i h_i[2(f_i-f_end)+||g_i||^2+2<g_i,x_start-x_i>]
          -||x_end-x_start||^2-B||g_end||^2.

For (left,alpha,right), child masses s,t, left endpoint a with gradientv, and final endpointm,

    H_Bnew(global)=H_Bleft(left)+H_Bright(right)
       +2(s+alpha)(f_a-f_m)+(Bleft+alpha-alpha^2)||v||^2
       +(Bright-Bnew)||g_m||^2.

With Q_ij=2(f_i-f_j)-2<g_j,x_i-x_j>-||g_i-g_j||^2>=0, the nonnegative combination

    2H_Bright+sum_right h_i(Q_ai+Q_mi)+Q_am+(alpha-1)Q_ma

equals

    2(t+2-alpha)(f_a-f_m)
      -(t+3alpha-2alpha^2)||v||^2-(t+2Bright+alpha)||g_m||^2.

Thus, if the right history functional is actually nonnegative and1<=alpha<t+2, this yields the corresponding generalized multistep decrease. It does NOT establish nonnegativity of the relaxed parent functional or composition of arbitrary endpoint potentials. Simply discarding child/cross residuals loses precisely information a new proof would need. No new universal dual-equivalence theorem was submitted or assumed.

## Next-action judgment and recovery

Do not rerun the exact q_j all-level candidate, the exact unit-positive-curvature two-sided family, common-normal replenishment covered by R6, or the unchanged rightmost-spine path product. Their proved scope now determines the next action. A future repair must provide an actual complete all-function certificate or an original-scale nonlinear witness, not another scalar assumption or equivalent selection functional.

The one-sided intermediate-radius sector is a concrete unresolved question; the final P_m is another. Neither is an established viable route. Full finite-cap asymmetric plateaus or genuinely coupled higher-dimensional histories remain possible, but require a new quantitative mechanism. No original-target proof or disproof was obtained, and no candidate merits a formal call yet.

All new research/programs require a fresh bounded allocation under standing human authority. There is no active native allocation after this closeout and no mathematical/formal credit carried forward. Goal remains ACTIVE/OPEN.

Primary-source checks used [Zhang--Jiang v2](https://arxiv.org/html/2410.12395v2) and [GSW v2, Proposition6 and appendix](https://arxiv.org/html/2410.16249v2). # R8/R8a direct research handoff: Goal ACTIVE, original target OPEN

2026-10-09 local time. Read current OPERATOR.md and original PROBLEM.md. The latest human priority is direct original-target closure inspired by Zhang--Jiang, with independent judgment and fewer equivalent formulations or fixed-horizon toys. ## Original target unchanged

For all finite-dimensional convex L-smooth functions with attained minimum, original selected minimizer/start and actual final iterate, use W_n(h)=sup2(f_n-f_star)/(L||x0-x_star||^2), W=2R. Prove lim W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1 uniformly over all sufficiently large horizons and EVERY predetermined nonnegative word, or give a certified full-function-class asymptotic separation. Exact benchmark C0=B0=1, C_n=max Omega(C_k,C_l), B_n=max G(C_k,B_l) over EVERY integer k+l=n-1, where Omega=(x+y+sqrt(x*x+6*x*y+y*y))/2, G=2x+y/2+sqrt(y*y+8*x*y)/2 and U_n^ZJ=1/B_n.

PROBLEM SHA3ecc3159a696e8528ebc727776d42f657cfbc6842e9212b28c870fcd05edb89d remains unchanged. The live Goal was ACTIVE/unbounded when R8 began. There is no user pause or external blocker. Mathematical incompleteness is not a reason to mark the Goal blocked or complete.

## Completed allocations and exact evidence status

All THREE calls finished; the parent read their COMPLETE proofs and handoffs and checked their final hashes. Mathematical programs, arrays, solvers and formal submissions were0 in every call. No additional models/agents were used by a native call. Parent added R8a under a fresh grant after deriving the area-preservation lead, so maximum live native concurrency was3 plus parent.

## Same hierarchy and accepted predecessor boundaries

For each enclosing m>=1536, hold eps=1/(4m), beta=1-eps, c=(1+sqrt2)(1+eps), a=beta/c fixed throughout H0 empty and H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j). Let S_j=sum H_j, D_m=1+2S_m, n_m=2^m-1. Accepted931b9653ce0f7201 gives B_(n_m)/D_m<47/50. The full-class hypothesis W(H_m)<=17/(16D_m) remains unproved; if proved for all sufficiently large m, it yields the strict gap799/800 and a negative resolution.

R6_QUADRATIC_BASELINE_BOUND_20261009.md remains an UNVERIFIED manual bound for the WHOLE convex-quadratic pool, <=9/(16D_m) for m>=4. Neither fact about this portfolio upper-bounds full nonlinear W, and it does not optimize every Huber crossing.

The parent freshly re-read the statement and complete Part B of accepted1e0bf3f41457d8e4; the matched-child call read statement and complete Parts B/C. It gives full finite-word projection-simplex supremum universality and the actual H3 all-body U_d law, with all starts and all d>=1, retaining the original polar minimum cone. This is a legitimate accepted base, not an all-depth theorem. Its Part B expressly avoids assuming primitive positivity even at H3.

## Actual tangent disk and quantitative sector bounds

For K=B(e1,1) in R2, define F_K(x)=max_(g in K)(<x,g>-||g||^2/2). It is one global convex1-smooth function with gradient P_K and complete minimum set{(z,0):z<=0}. For x=(1-u,v), u>=1, write r=sqrt(u^2+v^2), delta=r-u, q=r+u. The ORIGINAL closest minimum is(1-u0,0), so the initial radius squared is v0^2. Actual updates and objective are

    F=delta, g=(delta/r,v/r),
    u'=u+h*delta/r, v'=(1-h/r)*v,
    r'^2=r^2+2h*delta*(h/r-1), q'>=q,
    E=2F_end/v0^2=(2/q_end)*product(1-h_t/r_t)^2.

All radii in the product are coupled states of the one fixed function. The original-radius debt is ||x_t-x_star||^2=(r_t-u0)^2+2u0*delta_t. Supporting normals are exactly0.5(r_i-1)||g_i-g_j||^2, so old and future rows are genuine. Tangential curvature is1/r.

The native proves for EVERY complete finite nonnegative word with S=sum h>0 and b=max h, every left-side start with q0>=2b obeys

    E <=(2/q0)*exp(-2S/r0)<=1/(eS).

Here q monotonicity gives r_t>=b; consequently there is no reflection, r_t decreases, and every multiplier is controlled. On the exact H_m, S_m>3 gives D_m E<7/8 and hence B_n E<329/400. This controls the entire stated start sector. Failure of its sufficient condition does not imply that reflection occurs.

There is an explicit changing-curvature trajectory on every H_m: u0=v0=3S_m. Its r_t strictly decreases and q_t strictly increases at every physical step, and E>=4/(27sqrt2 S_m). This is only a subtarget constant, inside the deficient sector; it is not a viable target witness.

For each FIXED word and u0>=1, the limit v0->0 is exactly (1/u0)*product(1-h_t/u0)^2. It recovers the quadratic pool. Conditional on the R6 draft, fixed-m uniform continuity over1<=u0<=b_m gives some eta_m>0 with E<=3/(4D_m) when0<|v0|<=eta_m. No quantitative or uniform-in-m eta_m was proved.

The SAME disk already supplies the standard Huber witness on its positive horizontal axis. The function there is0 for z<=0, z^2/2 for0<=z<=2, and2z-2 forz>=2. The start x0=2D_m e1 has original closest minimum0 and payoff1/D_m. Because every H_m step exceeds1, every axis trajectory entering the quadratic interval before the terminal time reaches the minimum ray at its next step. Checking the possible final quadratic visit proves the exact optimum over ALL horizontal-axis starts is1/D_m. The radius v0^2 must not be used for a positive-axis start.

Still OPEN: finite-amplitude left starts with q0<2b_m, and positive-horizontal-coordinate nonaxis starts including their actual interior/exterior transitions. No whole-disk upper or target-scale lower was obtained. The optional vertex-plus-disk line was not used: a plateau already exists, and no quantified repair was available. The line stopped instead of producing another bare geometric parametrization.

## Area argument: correct invariant, but an actual positive-area bad sector

In p=(u,v) coordinates, T_h(p)=h e1+(1-h/r)p. Away from v=0 and r=h,

    det DT_h=1-h/r=v'/v,

so each chart(u,log|v|) has determinant1 and the measure du dv/|v| is preserved. The inverse branch is determined by sign(u'-h): for Rb=||p'-h e1||, r=h+Rb on u'>h and r=h-Rb on u'<h, with corresponding p=(1+h/Rb)(p'-h e1) or(1-h/Rb)(p'-h e1). The second branch needs Rb<h, and both must satisfy originalu>=1. Reflection changes the sign chart and causes no extra multiplicity.

The circle r=h collapses to(h,0), an actual minimum; first-hit preimages are null off the axis. The report gives the full image-domain bounds, not just a Jacobian. On any initial finite log-height band, the terminal region compatible with arbitrarily small payoff extends to log|v|=-infinity and has infinite invariant area. Positive preserved area alone therefore supplies no contradiction.

More decisively, the R8a report constructs an ACTUAL positive-area reflecting sector on every inherited H_m. Put ell_j=1+a*c^j, zeta=1/64, eta0=zeta^2/8, and choose

    p0=(ell0+delta)*(cos theta,sin theta),
    eta0*ell0/2 <=delta<=eta0*ell0,
    3/4<=cos theta<=4/5, sin theta>0.

It has u0>1, 4/5<|v0|<3, and invariant area

    (eta0*ell0/2)*integral_[acos(4/5),acos(3/4)] csc(theta)dtheta,

bounded below by a positive constant independent of m. The first leaf sends it exactly to(ell0+delta cos theta,delta sin theta). A complete-word bootstrap proves ell0<r_t<ell0*(1+zeta/2) thereafter. Thus every later leaf multiplier is<zeta and every nonleaf level j reflects with multiplier magnitude<=c^j. No point collapses.

The proof retains EVERY physical prefix through

    Q_j=zeta^(2^(j-1))*c^(2^j-j-1)
       =c^(-j-1)*d^(2^(j-1)), d=zeta*c^2<1/4,
    J_j=sum h_t*(formal prefix product)^2<3.

The exact recursion J_(j+1)=(1+A_j)J_j+ell_j Q_j^2, A_j=c^(2j)Q_j^2, and sum A_j<1/15 pays the actual horizontal drift and closes the uniform bootstrap. No array or numerical calculation was used. The ORIGINAL-radius payoff satisfies, at EVERY point of this sector,

    E <4^(-2^m),
    D_m*sup_sector E <11*3^m*4^(-2^m) ->0.

This is not a full-disk bound. It is a concrete reason not to treat area preservation, bounded initial radius and actual reflections as sufficient for target-scale selection. A new area argument would need quantitative control of the actual terminal image and its dependence on the original radius, outside this bad sector. No such selection estimate was found. Do not log the invariant itself as a minimax result.

## Matched-child U attempt: exact retained rows, no quantitative payment

For the actual two copies of H_j, let x_i,y_i and g_i=P(x_i), f_i=P(y_i), with y0=x_N-b*g_N. Put delta_i=x_i-y_i and e_i=g_i-f_i. The original matched normal sum is mu_i=<delta_i,e_i>-||e_i||^2>=0. It gives

    ||delta0||^2-||deltaN||^2
      =sum h_i(2-h_i)||e_i||^2+2sum h_i mu_i,

with actual bridge boundaries delta0=sum h_i g_i+b*g_N, deltaN=sum h_i f_i+b*g_N. The long-step terms have negative coefficients. Combining them with the accepted root U identity leaves the exact unpaid term in report(3.6), including the real right radial/maximization remainders with NEGATIVE signs. Their favorable values cannot be chosen independently.

The difference data are not automatically a convex gradient history. Their putative normal row is

    S_ij=Q_ij^(L,L)+Q_ij^(R,R)-Q_ij^(R,L)-Q_ij^(L,R)-mu_j,

which has no justified sign even though every original Q<=0. Thus no Silver child certificate was applied in a fictitious difference space.

The second line keeps the actual left auxiliary l=P(x/A), with A=1+2s, child capacityC=1+s, bridgeb=1+k, D=d+2s, h=C+k, M=D+2h, r=P(w/D), zL=[x-v+C p]/A and u=l-zL. Its parent point is

    z_theta=zP+(theta*A/M)u,
    M z_theta=(1-theta)sum h_i g_i+(b-theta C)p+(D+h)r+theta A l.

Convexity proves feasibility for0<=theta<=b/C<1. The exact parent gap(4.5) contains actual child U gaps and nonnegative root/parent reserves, but also

    T_theta=-(1-theta)^2 A^2||u||^2
       -2(1-theta)A<x-A l,u>
       +2theta A<(s-k)p-(D+h)r,u>.

The normal<x-A l,u> is nonnegative but has a negative coefficient. Taking theta1 cancels an earlier remainder coefficient yet makes bridge weight b-C=k-s negative; no actual same-body membership of the extrapolated point was proved. The root square controls p-r but not the leftover r component. The positive root coefficient itself is not the problem: Theta>3C(C+k)/46 at all relevant higher levels. No all-depth certificate, uniform controlled loss, or actual counterexample to U was obtained.

These two signed identities identify an unpaid term; they are not a replacement theorem of equal strength and do not establish minimax progress by themselves. The next upper attempt needs an actual quantitative payment with all correlated child data.

## Research judgment and unallocated leads

Do not renew the bare area-preservation argument, rederive the disk recurrence, retune the already bounded nonreflecting/axis sectors, assume a difference-space objective, or declare theta1 feasible from convexity. None supplies the original target.

A more promising lower search, not yet allocated or proved, is the complete NONVERTEX evolution in a fixed weighted centered simplex. On a finite probability space with positive fixed weights p_i, use the mean-zero L2 space and K={g:E g=0,g_i>=-1}. This is a compact centered simplex. Projection has g_i=(X_i-t)_+-1, where t is uniquely chosen by E(X-t)_+=1. Thus one fixed function gives the actual update X'=X-h*g, preserving E X=0 and the original squared radius E X0^2. Its objective is F=t+1/2+E(X-t)_+^2/2. These parent manual starting formulas require independent checking before use; they are NOT a new lower bound or a universality theorem for arbitrary functions.

The potential advantage is simultaneous nonvertex face mixtures with fixed atom weights, rather than the vertex-by-vertex itineraries previously bounded. The starting law must be selected ONCE from the full prescribed word, and then all physical steps propagated without resetting atoms or child states. A useful new call must actually choose such a finite law and prove the original-scale payoff, first on the inherited family and then uniformly on all words. Merely outputting this probability reformulation would repeat the old gap. The existing R5 centered-simplex construction and R6/R7 restrictions are prerequisites, not proofs about this full nonvertex law.

For an upper attempt, one unallocated possibility is to quantify what fraction of an ACTUAL child maximization remainder is retained by its U gap. The relation E_L=V_L/A+RL suggests testing a genuinely proved relative-debt bound before trying another scalar terminal profile. No coefficient, base case or composition theorem for such a bound is known. It must not be assumed, and a clipped/extrapolated comparison point must have actual membership and all correction terms proved. These are directions, not results or renewed grants.

The parent also considered an interior shifted disk B(b e1,1),0<b<1, whose unique minimum and reversible horizontal drift differ from the tangent-ray geometry. No trajectory/payoff theorem or allocation was made for it. Its existence alone is not evidence that it can close the target.

## Primary-source refresh and operating state

A bounded new primary-source search did not supply a theorem resolving this exact minimax profile. This was not a comprehensive absence-of-literature claim. The parent inspected [Peppy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35762), posted September28, and its [Sections2.3--2.5 and AppendicesC/D](https://arxiv.org/html/2609.35762). It extracts low-rank partial-sum Lyapunov structure from full PEP certificates and then completes analytic identities. The inspected examples concern specified algorithms such as FGM; no solution to the present Silver minimax question was imported. No Peppy code, model workflow, solver or old numerical array was run. Its method is only a possible guide to a future freshly bounded certificate search.

Initial cached gm_search metadata extraction used the wrong nesting and returned nulls; the cached result was then parsed correctly without another RPC. A broad cached print was truncated; only the relevant public claim/evidence record was copied, and the native read that complete copy. A guessed R3 handoff alias was absent; the current R7 handoff and actual accepted Part B/C were used instead. No private local-memory link was followed. There was no approval-review rejection.

All R8/R8a work is closed after the receipt. Original Goal remains ACTIVE/OPEN, with no complete candidate for formal verification and no mathematical/program/formal allowance carried forward. Any next call requires a fresh bounded grant under the standing human authorization.

## Fixed finite-law geometry, with original radius

On a finite space with positive fixed probabilities p_i, work in E={X:E X=0} with norm squared E X^2. The isometry X_i->sqrt(p_i)X_i makes this an ordinary finite-dimensional Euclidean problem. Let K={g:E g=0,g_i>=-1}; it is a compact centered simplex with0 in its relative interior. Its projection envelope F_K(X)=max_(g in K)(E[Xg]-E[g^2]/2) is globally convex1-smooth with unique minimum0.

The actual projection and physical update are

    g_i=(X_i-t)_+-1, E(X-t)_+=1,
    X_i'=X_i-h*g_i, E X'=0,
    F_K(X)=t+1/2+E(X-t)_+^2/2.

The threshold is unique and t>=-1. Original radius squared is E X0^2. Probability is only coordinate bookkeeping: this is ONE deterministic finite-dimensional function and trajectory, not an averaged performance claim over changing functions. Atom weights, function and initial values are fixed before the run. Backward atom splitting duplicates identical current/future values and therefore preserves every already-built future threshold and objective. All old/cross/terminal rows then come from the same projection body.

This complete nonvertex evolution is broader than the earlier one-vertex-per-phase constructions. No universality of this narrower simplex family for arbitrary functions is assumed.

## Actual laws that failed, with complete quantitative scope

All diagnostic H_m below use the SAME enclosing eps=1/(4m), beta=1-eps, c=(1+sqrt2)(1+eps), a=beta/c in every child H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j). Let S_j=sum H_j, D_m=1+2S_m, n_m=2^m-1. Accepted931b9653ce0f7201 gives B_n/D_m<47/50 for every m>=1536; its full-class upper W(H_m)<=17/(16D_m) remains unproved.

The first R9 law has exactly64 equal-weight midpoint atoms in[-h0^2,h0^2], h0=1+a. A sup-norm transfer from the exactly folded continuous uniform law proves that its first actual output lies in[-57/64,57/64]. Every subsequent prefix remains in the quadratic core. With P_m=beta^(2^m-1)c^(-m), the law's payoff is<9P_m^2 and D_m times its payoff tends to0. This is a result for this specific finite law, not every law or every nonvertex trajectory.

An independent inverse-branch policy starting from terminal+/-2 is also completely specified. At each reversed h>1, d=h-1 and q=maxY, take a_*=q+d and omega=d/(q+d), independent of Y. It gives a finite fixed law with multiple active atoms at every preterminal time, but its original variance is too large. If current support is[-L,q],

    L_prev=L+h, q_prev=(hq+L)/d,
    V_prev=[(q+1/d)/(q+d)]V+h^2q/d.

On H_m its payoff is<(9/8)*3^(-2^(m-1)), again o(1/D_m). This only rejects that deliberately nonoptimized independent allocation, not the optimized inverse mechanism.

## Constructive inverse laws and exact one-step optimization

For any finite mean-zero next law Y and h>1, d=h-1, choose a_*>=max(d,maxY), Z=a_*-Y, and conditional right probability omega(Y) satisfying E[omega Z]=d. The two actual preimages are

    X_left=Y-h, X_right=a_*-h+Z/d.

They have mean0 and physical projection threshold a_*-h. Both branches map exactly toY under the original h. Their original variance is

    V(X)=V(Y)+2h a_*-h^2+(d^-2-1)J,
    J=E[omega Z^2].

At fixed a_*, minimizing this variance is fractional knapsack: fill highest Y for1<h<2 and lowestY for h>2. At most one atom needs a fractional split. The threshold optimization is also exact. On an interval with fractional cutoff z and fully selected setA,

    J'(a_*)=d+E[1_A(z-Y)].

For h>=2 the optimized variance increases strictly with a_*, so its minimum is at max(d,maxY). For1<h<2 the value is convex piecewise affine, with slope

    h^2/d-(d^-2-1)E(Y-z)_+.

Its minimum is at the lower endpoint or a feasible upper-tail breakpointa_*=(d+E[Y1_A])/P(A). This identifies one-step useful thresholds without a solver. Equal current variance does NOT make resulting tail laws interchangeable for earlier physical steps.

The inverse is also explicit for every remaining nonnegative step. For0<h<1, choose a_* byE(Y-a_*)_+=1-h and use X=Y-h+[h/(1-h)](Y-a_*)_+. Forh1, with a_*=maxY and p=P(Y=a_*), take X=Y-1 offmax and X=a_*-1+1/p onmax. Forh0, useidentity. Thus a definite greedy reverse rule can use at mostn+2 atoms, all fixed before execution. Existence of that law is not the required performance estimate.

## A genuine link to the Zhang--Jiang retained potential

For1<h<2, set d=h-1 and gamma=dh/(2-h). Let z be the actual cutoff E(Y-z)_+=gamma, and select the upper tail abovez with total massp, optionally using boundary mass atz. Set a_*=z+(d+gamma)/p. Its EXACT real support guard is

    p(maxY-z)<=d+gamma,

equivalently the maximum active excess divided by its conditional mean is<=2/h. When this guard holds, the constructed optimized predecessor satisfies

    V(X)=V(Y)+2h gamma F_K(Y/gamma)
                   +[h^2/(2-h)]||P_K(Y/gamma)||^2.

At h=sqrt2, gamma1 and this is exactly

    V(X)=V(Y)+2sqrt2 F_K(Y)+(2+sqrt2)||P_KY||^2.

This is an ACTUALLY ATTAINED one-step relation under a stated guard, not just a formal potential. The evaluations atY/gamma remain auxiliary samples of the SAME body; no physical word is rescaled or restarted.

The actual inherited leaf has gamma0=h0*a/(1-a)<1. A concrete backward test from terminal+/-2 makes a three-atom law at the level1 bridge: after the preceding inverse leaf let r=2+h0; the bridge produces

    u=r-(1+beta)+2r/beta, massbeta/(2r),
    v=r-(1+beta), mass1/2,
    w=-r-(1+beta), mass(r-beta)/(2r).

Its stop-loss atv is exactly1. The next leaf's gamma0 cutoff lies STRICTLY abovev, so only the top atom is selected and no boundary mass can be varied at equal cost. The reverse update returns to a vertex step. More generally, a cap bridge selecting only the minimum atom regenerates this same stop-loss1 gap. A genuinely nonvertex closure needs multiple distinct selected lower values or a different boundary-mass family, and must propagate the real2/h excess-concentration guard. No such all-level propagation was proved.

There is also a sharp obstruction after optimizing a_* and omega. Forh2, minimum predecessor variance isV(Y)+4max(1,maxY)-4. The lawY=A with probability1/(A^2+1), Y=-1/A otherwise, hasV(Y)=2F(Y)=1 but minimum predecessor variance4A-3. Symmetric+/-1 has the same variance/value and minimum predecessor variance1. Thus those two scalar terminal quantities alone lose arbitrarily much; tail geometry cannot be discarded. This does not refute optimized inversion on H_m.

## Parent correction: fixed terminal plus/minus2 is insufficient even without greedy choices

Read R9_PARENT_INVERSE_COST_NOTE_20261009.md before continuing. For unit steps on any smooth convex function, it proves

    ||x0-xstar||^2 >= ||xn-xstar||^2
                  +2n(f_n-fstar)+n(n+1)||g_n||^2.

A terminal law+/-2 has squared norm4, gap3/2, gradient squared norm1. Therefore EVERY admissible preimage with that same terminal law has original variance at least(n+2)^2. The two-atom noncrossing trajectory attains equality. Its exact best fixed-terminal payoff is3/(n+2)^2=o(1/B_n), using accepted931b B_n<=(12/5)(n+1)^rho withrho<2.

Accordingly, the native report's standalone fixed-terminal estimate V0<=3B_n/(1-o1) is REFUTED on unit-step words, not merely unproved. Its specific inherited-H_m estimate remains open, and standardHuber already handles the unit-step words in a combined portfolio. This correction does not reject the general inverse-law method.

The parent also supplies the exact benchmark-scaled terminal choice

    Q_n=(B_n+1)/2, terminalY=+/-Q_n, terminal2F=B_n.

For every word of total massS<=(B_n-1)/2, the actual noncrossing start+/-(Q_n+S) has variance<=(B_n)^2 and proves W>=1/B_n exactly. This covers the entire low-mass case, including zero steps. For larger total masses a natural sufficient goal is a law with the same terminal value and original variance<=(1+o1)B_n^2; neither that estimate nor sufficiency of a two-point terminal shape is assumed.

## Parent extension: optimize the preceding prefix cost, not just one-step variance

The parent note derives a conditional attained identity for local cost coefficients A,B>=0 (not benchmarkB_n). For the same inverse law,

    C_(A,B)(X)=V(X)+2A F_K(X)+B||P_KX||^2
      =V(Y)+2(h+A)a_*-h^2-2Ah+A-B
              +[(1+A+B)/d^2-1]J.

Thus the relevant allocation sign is1+A+B-d^2. A choice minimizing only current variance may increase the cost of preceding physical steps.

IfC0=1+A+B>d^2, putT=h+A, w=C0/d^2-1, gamma=d+2T/w. With a real cutoff E(Y-z)_+=gamma, selected upper massp and guardp(maxY-z)<=d+gamma, the actual construction attains

    B_gamma=w gamma^2-T gamma=gamma(wd+T)>0,
    U_gamma(Y)=2gamma F_K(Y/gamma).

All constants cancel exactly, as shown in the parent note. This is not a closed multi-step theorem: the auxiliary-threshold cost and support guard still must be propagated on the same physical law. No finite-law terminal payoff at the target scale has been established on H_m, let alone on arbitrary high-mass words.

The primitive joining root also appears in this attained inverse cost. For A=C_L-1, B=C_L(C_L-1), h=1+d with0<d<C_L, one obtains gamma=d(C_L+d)/(C_L-d). This exact scalar match does not establish joint attainability of the two children or preserve the guard through a whole physical word.

# R5 activation handoff

Status: **UNVERIFIED. Original exact-profile target OPEN.** One fresh Astra/ultra analytical invocation completed; two substantive lines; mathematical programs/arrays/scans/solvers 0; formal submissions 0; additional models/agents 0. Parent owns ledger and any future grant.

Read PROOF_ATTEMPT.md for complete derivations and scope. The performance normalization is exactly \(\mathcal W=2\mathcal R\), and the benchmark remains \(B_n=2S_\bullet(n)+1\), with every integer split. The proof separately uses \(R^2\) for the initial squared radius. No claim of completion, asymptotic disproof, or new accepted fact was made.

1. **Silent ridge bank.** Independent fresh normals force the full global curvature sum, even if their quadratic regions are visited at different times. A nonnegative convex bank zero at the original minimizer and every old query is zero on their full convex hull. The first-activation estimate is \(2H(x-hg)\le h^2g^T(I-A)g\). If the old collinear direction has curvature \(1-\delta\), every such bank satisfies the SHARP transverse bound \(\|P_\perp\nabla H(-re)\|\le r\sqrt\delta/2\). The bound is attained by an explicit two-dimensional static function with closest original minimizer 0 and radius 1. This does not bound arbitrary future amplification or the whole old objective.

2. **Actual high-dimensional Huber relay.** Equations (10)--(22) specify one finite-dimensional static function for any finite number of checkpoints, prove its global smoothness, complete minimum set and exact original radius, and track ALL actual regions under explicit return guards. The exact terminal witness includes both the minimum-preserving first-crossing subtraction \(b_0-1/d\) and the spectral coupling coefficient \((c/d)^{2K}\). Dropping either is invalid. All ordinary entries, empty blocks and zero steps are accounted for.

3. **Decisive constant check inside line 2.** For the ENTIRE admissible central one-transfer relay family, with equal side sums \(s\),

   \[
   E/H_{\rm base} < (b/s)^2(2+b/s+1/(2s)).
   \]

   On the inherited-m, parameter-1/4 Gray word of the supplied report, \(b/s\to\sqrt2-1\), giving a uniform limsup at most \(\sqrt2-1<1\). This subfamily cannot repair that word's known Huber gap. The parent freshly confirmed accepted fact 5fd396eba75651e7 for that Huber comparison and two specified spiral families. It does not certify the complete quadratic pool; no such assertion was added.

No all-word checkpoint selection or constant-preserving Zhang--Jiang recursion follows from this relay. Such a missing result cannot be treated as routine. The central one-transfer family is closed for this route; merely changing its d, c or cap scale is already covered by the analytic bound. Many-transfer relays and other static functions are not excluded, but would require a genuinely new closure argument, including every backward return.

No mathematical program, recurrence replay, numerical experiment, formal submission, private worker-memory read, service action or truth-store edit was performed. The artifacts are drafts, not verifier-accepted authority. Stop this invocation here; no automatic renewal.
